- **intake**: You are the intake interviewer of the Archon `factory` workflow. Your job is to draft the project profile that every later step will rely on. ([OlegShevtsov1/1_mvp_sdlc](https://github.com/OlegShevtsov1/1_mvp_sdlc/blob/HEAD/orchestration/factory/.archon/workflows/sdlc-factory/factory/commands/intake.md))

- **intake**: Build _/tracks/&lt;TICKET&gt;.md — the single source of truth for one ticket. ([RemGrace/rem-sdlc](https://github.com/RemGrace/rem-sdlc/blob/HEAD/commands/intake.md))
- **intake**: Stage 0 intake - scan images/0.Originals for new files (stability gate), run lw_pipeline intake per image (dry-run first, then execute), kick off the source-recovery waterfall (Tier 0 local pHash, Tier 1 DeviantArt token decode + gallery-dl, Tier 2 SauceNAO, else manual queue), and record provenance in each image manifest. Use when new wallpaper sources have been dropped and the operator says "intake" or "start intake". ([Remus3/Legion-Wallpaper](https://github.com/Remus3/Legion-Wallpaper/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/intake.md))

- **intake**: Étape 4 d'une campagne : relit en critique les rapports Claude Research déposés par l'humain, tranche affirmation par affirmation ce qui sera repris, ouvre la liste de comblement du sujet dans la carte, puis comble par les canaux d'une session Claude Code. Un rapport est une source de plus, pas un acquis : la liste s'écrit avant toute collecte, et un trou qu'aucun canal ne comble se déclare irréductible plutôt que de disparaître. Étape terminale d'une campagne de thème, dont le livrable est le corpus. ([sebc-dev/marketplace](https://github.com/sebc-dev/marketplace/blob/HEAD/scd-atlas/.claude/commands/intake.md))

- **intersect.ar**: كيف يعمل أمر ⁨INTERSECT⁩ في أوتوكاد: مجسمات ثلاثية الأبعاد، أو مناطق في مستوى واحد، تُختزل إلى ما تشترك فيه، ويُحذف الباقي. جرّبه مجانًا في متصفحك. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.ar.md))
- **intersect.cs**: Jak funguje PRŮNIK (INTERSECT) v AutoCADu: ze dvou nebo více 3D těles nebo oblastí v jedné rovině zůstane jen společná část, zbytek zmizí. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.cs.md))
- **intersect.de**: Wie SCHNITTMENGE (INTERSECT, Alias SM) in AutoCAD funktioniert: nur das behalten, was 3D-Volumenkörper oder Regionen gemeinsam haben. Zum Testen im Browser. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.de.md))
- **intersect.es**: Cómo funciona INTERSEC (INTERSECT, alias INT) de AutoCAD en español: sólidos 3D, o regiones de un plano, reducidos a lo que comparten. Pruébalo gratis. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.es.md))
- **intersect.fr**: Comment fonctionne INTERSECT (alias IR) d'AutoCAD en français : des solides 3D, ou des régions d'un plan, réduits à ce qu'ils partagent. Essayez-le en ligne. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.fr.md))
- **intersect.hu**: Az AutoCAD KÖZÖSRÉSZ (INTERSECT, KO) parancsa: két vagy több 3D szilárdtestből vagy egy síkban fekvő lemezekből csak a közös rész marad, a többi törlődik. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.hu.md))
- **intersect.it**: Come funziona INTERSEZIONE (INTERSECT, alias IT) di AutoCAD in italiano: due o più solidi 3D, o regioni in un piano, ridotti a ciò che condividono. Provalo. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.it.md))
- **intersect.ja**: AutoCAD の交差(INTERSECT、IN)の使い方。2 つ以上の 3D ソリッド、または同じ平面上のリージョンを、すべてに共通する部分だけにして残りを削除します。ブラウザで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.ja.md))
- **intersect.ko**: AutoCAD 교집합(INTERSECT, IN) 사용법: 둘 이상의 3D 솔리드나 같은 평면의 영역에서 모두가 공유하는 부분만 남기고 나머지는 지웁니다. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.ko.md))
- **intersect.pl**: Jak działa ILOCZYN (INTERSECT) w AutoCADzie: z dwóch lub więcej brył 3D albo regionów w jednej płaszczyźnie zostaje tylko część wspólna, reszta znika. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.pl.md))
- **intersect.pt-BR**: Como funciona o INTERSECT do AutoCAD em português, INTERSEC (alias INT): sólidos 3D, ou regiões de um plano, reduzidos ao que têm em comum. Experimente grátis. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.pt-BR.md))
- **intersect.ru**: Как работает ПЕРЕСЕЧЕНИЕ (INTERSECT) в AutoCAD: из двух и более 3D-тел или областей в одной плоскости остается только их общая часть, остальное стирается. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.ru.md))
- **intersect.zh-CN**: CAD 交集怎么用：AutoCAD 的 INTERSECT（快捷键 IN）将两个或多个三维实体，或同一平面中的面域，修剪为它们的公共部分，其余部分删除。在浏览器中免费试用。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.zh-CN.md))
